open-gsd/gsd-core ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.20.1 Stable2026-11-05T14:29:14Z

Install

npm i @opengsd/gsd-core@1.20.1
# or
npm i @opengsd/gsd-core@latest

What's Changed

Fixed

  • The fork-probe cache stays inside the project and out of git — the cache no longer writes through a symlinked .gsd that points outside the project, and a fresh project no longer shows .gsd/ as untracked or sweeps it into git add -A. (#5757) (#5908)
  • Installs into a nested or unusually named config directory keep correct paths on every runtime — the installer rewrote ~/.claude references in several passes, and the converters that run afterwards (agents, the gsd-core/ spec tree, OpenCode and Kilo commands and skills, command bodies, the Codex agent TOML) scanned the emitted prefix again, so a --config-dir such as ~/.claude/profiles/work, ~/.claude.d or ~/.copilot/work was rewritten inside its own output, and $& in a config dir was expanded into the text around it. A Kilo install wrote paths like .../profiles/work/profiles/work/gsd-core, an OpenCode install wrote paths like .claude/~/.config/opencode/x, and a Codex agent TOML got ~/.codex in place of $&. Each reference is now rewritten once and the emitted prefix is shielded from every later converter, for Kilo, OpenCode, Codex, Cline, CodeBuddy, Hermes, Qwen, Windsurf, Kimi, Trae agents and spec tree, Augment commands, and the Copilot and Antigravity @ includes for a config dir under their own default home. Intentional changes to default-install output: (1) Windsurf global installs turned ~/.devin/skills/x and $HOME/.devin/skills/x into ~/$HOME/.codeium/windsurf/skills/x and $HOME/$HOME/...; they now give $HOME/.codeium/windsurf/skills/x. (2) Claude Code's own files such as ~/.claude.json, $HOME/.claude.json, ~/.claude-plugin and ./.claude-plugin were rewritten into nonexistent names like $HOME/.cline.json; they now stay unchanged. (3) A Trae local install wrote $HOME/.trae/... and ~/.trae paths, including @$HOME/.trae/gsd-core/... includes, into its skills, which point at the global directory; they now point at the project's .trae/. A comparison of real default installs of all 18 installable runtimes, global and local, against the previous build found no other difference in the installed files. (#5715) (#5906)
  • effort sync --apply no longer makes the next install back up synced agents as local patches — the sync rewrote agent files without updating gsd-file-manifest.json, so the next install treated every synced agent as a local modification; the manifest now records the synced files. (#5683) (#5899)
  • Two orchestrators in one checkout no longer deny each other's sequential dispatches — when parallel workstreams ran execute-phase in the same checkout, each recorded isolation decision overwrote the other's, so a plan resolved to run without a worktree was refused by the isolation guard; each dispatch's decision is now kept for its own phase and plan. (#5682) (#5891)
  • drift-guard phase-status and workstream progress read milestone-prefixed phases correctly — under phase_id_convention: milestone-prefixed every M-NN phase named in ROADMAP Progress rows or STATE.md read as the bare milestone, so phase-status compared STATE against the milestone's first phase and reported a false drift, and workstream progress collapsed the milestone's phases into one. (#5675) (#5887)
  • phase insert names the milestone when the phase belongs to another one — inserting after a phase that lives in a different milestone than the current one reported Phase N not found in ROADMAP.md, the same text as a phase missing from the file; the error now names the milestone that holds it and the current one. (#5679) (#5893)
  • new-project and new-milestone point to the right next step — the closing hand-off said /gsd-plan-phase starts execution; it now recommends /gsd-discuss-phase, with /gsd-plan-phase to plan directly and /gsd-execute-phase to execute. (#5723) (#5905)
  • phase complete, state sync and init progress read milestone-prefixed phase ids correctly — under phase_id_convention: milestone-prefixed, re-completing a phase such as 3-01 while STATE.md was at 3-02 rewound STATE to the completed phase, a first-time completion advanced STATE to phase 3 named "02: beta", and roadmap checklist rows, phase headings and phase directories such as 03-02-beta were not recognised by init progress, init manager (its checked-phase fallback), find-phase (which reported phase 03 named "02-beta") and the planning snapshot. (#5676) (#5896)

Full Changelog: https://github.com/open-gsd/gsd-core/compare/v1.20.0...v1.20.1

v2.0.0Context Mode 2.0 Stable2026-11-04T17:08:00Z

Context Mode 2.0

Context Mode is a free, local, open source plugin for 10 coding agents. The agent writes a short script, the script runs in a local sandbox, and only the answer enters the context window. The full output stays in a SQLite index on your machine, so the agent can search it later.

2.0 is a full rewrite: 9 tools, hooks on all 10 agents, one database per project, and a bench you can run yourself.

Upgrade from 1.x

Use your agent's own command. Then restart the agent and run /ctx-upgrade in it: it replaces the old 1.x entries and keeps your own settings. /ctx-doctor then shows PASS or a fix for each check.

Agent Install or update
Claude Code claude plugin marketplace add mksglu/context-mode then claude plugin install context-mode@context-mode
Codex codex plugin marketplace add mksglu/context-mode then codex plugin add context-mode@context-mode, and once: npm i -g context-mode and context-mode setup --agents codex (trusts the plugin hooks)
Gemini CLI gemini extensions install https://github.com/mksglu/context-mode (update: gemini extensions update context-mode)
Qwen Code qwen extensions install https://github.com/mksglu/context-mode:context-mode (update: qwen extensions update context-mode)
Pi pi install npm:context-mode (update: pi update --extensions)
OpenCode opencode plugin add context-mode (update: opencode plugin update)
Copilot CLI npm i -g context-mode then context-mode setup --agents copilot-cli
Kiro npm i -g context-mode then context-mode setup --agents kiro
VS Code (Copilot) npm i -g context-mode then context-mode setup --agents vscode
ZCode npm i -g context-mode then context-mode setup --agents zcode

Every block, with check and remove steps: README, Install and the Install guide.

Highlights

  • Thinking in Code. The agent sends a script, and only what it prints comes back. On 5,000 JSON records (1,039,898 B) the agent got 254 B. How it works, step 5
  • Sandbox tools. ctx_execute runs shell, JavaScript, TypeScript, Python and other runtimes in the project folder; ctx_batch runs many commands, indexes the output and searches it in one call. Tools
  • 99.4% of output bytes saved. Median over the 36 bench rows with 10 KB or more of raw output, 5 runs per row. Bench file
  • Search. Each chunk goes into a Porter-stemmed FTS5 table and a trigram table. ctx_search ranks each with BM25 and joins them with Reciprocal Rank Fusion (k = 60), with a spelling fix from the indexed words. 20 queries: 1.62 ms median. How it works, step 7
  • One search for content and memory. ctx_search reads the index and every agent's memory files (such as AGENTS.md) in one call. How it works, step 7
  • Run, then replace. On hosts that run the tool first, output over 4,096 B goes into the index whole, and the agent gets the best chunks. One 666,796 B log put 3,380 B into the context. Longer sessions
  • Session continuity. The hooks keep a journal of prompts, edits, commands and errors. After compaction, ctx_resume brings back a state block of at most 2,048 B: a 442,893 B session came back in 2,019 B. Long sessions guide
  • A full session. One scripted debugging session: 281,261 B of raw output, 3,266 B in the context. Bench file
  • ctx_stats report. Shows how much output stayed out of the context, this session and all-time. Tools
  • ctx_doctor with issue reports. One row per check, each with PASS or a fix command. When a check still fails, doctor writes a report file and a ready gh issue command for the agent to show you. Doctor guide
  • 10 agents with hooks. Claude Code, Codex, Gemini CLI, Qwen Code, Pi, OpenCode, Copilot CLI, Kiro, VS Code and ZCode. A hook adds 7.5 ms to 12.3 ms over a bare node start. Agents guide
  • Node's own SQLite. The store uses the built-in node:sqlite: no native module to build, no Bun. How it works, step 10
  • Windows. CI runs setup, doctor, a hook and an MCP handshake for all 10 agents on Windows, Linux and macOS. WINDOWS.md
  • A bench you can run. cd bench && node run.mjs runs offline with plain Node and writes the same tables. bench/README.md, Benchmarks guide

Changes for 1.x users

<details><summary>What changed and how to move</summary>

  • Node 24.15.0 or later. 1.x needed Node 22.5. 2.0 uses node:sqlite, and 24.15.0 is the first version with it unflagged. Bun and better-sqlite3 are gone.
  • Data store. Each project has one context.db under ~/.context-mode. 2.0 starts a new index and does not copy 1.x data. ctx_doctor shows where the 1.x data is and its size, and nothing gets deleted without asking you.
  • Setup. The CLI has three commands: context-mode setup, context-mode doctor and context-mode upgrade. Setup shows a plan, saves your config, then applies the plan; it also replaces 1.x entries. The 1.x hook forms (context-mode hook <platform> <event> and mcp) still run, and doctor asks you to run setup to rewrite them.
  • Tools. Nine tools: ctx_execute, ctx_batch, ctx_index, ctx_search, ctx_resume, ctx_stats, ctx_doctor, ctx_upgrade, ctx_purge.
    • ctx_execute_file is now ctx_execute with a file field.
    • ctx_batch_execute is now ctx_batch.
    • ctx_fetch_and_index is now ctx_index with a url field.
    • ctx_insight and context-mode insight are gone.
  • Removed in 2.0: statusline, HTTP daemon mode, the CLI index and search commands, routing files in your project (CLAUDE.md, AGENTS.md templates). The routing now lives in the tool descriptions, so setup never writes your instruction files.
  • Agents. 2.0 supports 10 agents. Cursor, JetBrains Copilot, Kimi, Codex Desktop, OpenClaw, OMP (Oh My Pi), Antigravity (agy) and Zed are not in 2.0. To keep one of them, stay on 1.0.169.
  • Your settings stay. Setup and /ctx-upgrade keep your own hooks, hook timeouts, permissions.deny and MCP entries.

The step to move: run your agent's command from the table above, restart the agent, run /ctx-upgrade, then /ctx-doctor. If you installed 1.x with npm, remove the old global package only after that: npm uninstall -g context-mode comes last, because 1.x hooks call the global command until /ctx-upgrade replaces them.

</details>

Features and fixes

This part covers 307 commits since 1.0.169: 72 features, 222 fixes and 13 performance changes.

Search

<details><summary>15 changes</summary>

  • ctx_search ranks every agent's memory files together with indexed content, and a source filter that names a memory file searches that file. 4415e2f7 5ef0a2a6
  • Search ranks in layers: stopwords, chunks that match every term first, then an OR fallback when hits are few. ba1b9b7a 60e1b62f
  • Search uses porter stemming and a chunk title column. c1fd04f3
  • Search adds a trigram table, RRF fusion and typo correction from the index vocabulary. f8f7850e
  • Markdown chunks split on headings and keep the title path; code fences never split, and inline content with a heading or fence chunks as markdown. 697b13a1 7a26490f
  • Fetched HTML turns into readable markdown before chunking. 7b47de82
  • Snippets are verbatim, fence-aware and chosen by score; a snippet takes a whole neighbour fence, and typo words place it by prefix. 62ac32c2 de09a613
  • A relevance reply holds 5 hits, and More: pages the rest with one line per cursor. c312684e c028484a
  • Replies are lean when the top hit holds every query word, and smaller on small sources with no empty query blocks. c028484a 0841fda6
  • A search over a JSON source returns whole BM25-ranked rows. 30c84f3a
  • A source query with no matching words lists that source's chunks by position. ce89bfc4
  • A stored label wins over a handle; a long label shows as handle, size and preview. a621d67d
  • A NUL in a query splits words and gives a normal result. eb523586
  • Chunks keep a line over the small cap whole, so search returns the whole line. 6fefd968
  • Search typo correction, scoped ranking and prepared statements run faster (perf). d36b505e e52388ac 36ebb21b

</details>

Sandbox (ctx_execute / ctx_batch)

  • ctx_execute gives a bare reply with the whole output on exit 0, and a 4 KB preview when output is stored. 06535f10
  • ctx_batch replies show the exit code only when it is not zero and a pointer only when output was left out. d4c98b3c
  • Each ctx_batch call answers from its own sources first, under its own label ctx_batch:<turn>:<n>. 5dd8a04d
  • The mvn.cmd rewrite from #782 is back. 4fe21f7d
  • Every tool description starts with an exact example call; a bad call gets one line with the correct call on all hosts. b66edb3c
  • Server instructions and the ctx_execute description are shorter, so each session starts with fewer tokens (perf). c70f1e20

Hooks and routing

<details><summary>16 changes</summary>

  • The hook never errors: all 10 hosts share one fallback decision. 6de98259
  • The shell-dump deny checks every command in a chain, follows cd and unwraps zsh -c. 9fba2c52
  • The policy scans command substitutions inside $(( )). 8e7edda9
  • Unbounded PowerShell file dumps (Get-Content, gc, type, [IO.File]::ReadAllText) get the same deny as cat. 765be33d
  • The Windows deny glob uses forward slashes and a drive-less // anchor. 872216b8
  • The Read deny is one line with the exact ctx_execute call for the file. 6c9f8a37
  • The main agent runs the tool, then the hook replaces large output with the best chunks. 3f4a4d0b 16df63bd
  • The replace ranks chunks by the call's intent or the last user prompt words; with no description, intent comes from the command, path, pattern or URL. 8db7170f ae0a3169
  • The replace shapes output by content type, reads the whole stored Bash output, and the digest header carries counts only. 85c34f06 41eb547a ed0108c3
  • Non-ctx MCP results and Grep content results get the same replace; one-off categorical rows count as rare units. 90e3dce9 37cc74b0
  • JSON output gets a digest header with the schema and numeric extremes for a named key, inside its own 256 B budget. 94332734 2ebdf842
  • The replacement names the stored source by short id, size and the search call to run. 4bf03c9c c98b71a6 cccb08ca 5e4a9737 d5c1302c
  • The handle line adds the prompt words the output holds as a second query. 7e4d3975
  • Replace output stays clean at an empty body and the selection budget stops at 0. 0a4a77de fef78434
  • Hook texts name tools with each host's own tool prefix on all 10 hosts. 054eb84e
  • The hook reads stdin as a stream in its entry. 693403b2

</details>

  • The MCP entry goes to the user-profile mcp.json; doctor reads the user hook file setup writes. 38c191ee db09aee6
  • Skills move to ~/.agents/skills. b195b992

ZCode

  • Setup writes matcher groups and mcp.servers/features.mcp; uninstall drops the nested entry. 6be8e768

</details>

Windows

<details><summary>13 changes</summary>

  • Home is USERPROFILE first on Windows and HOME first elsewhere, from one helper; doctor names USERPROFILE when no home is found. 7b54a2ed d6a0648d 8c9d87ad
  • File writes retry a busy rename (EPERM, EBUSY, EACCES), name the held file and clean their tmp file. d7886972 31c21f76 567970c2 3874f2cf 49db0498 ee95ebb9
  • Setup finds agent CLIs via PATHEXT and runs .cmd shims through cmd.exe with /v:off. 6fcc3112 76029ea7 37db76fe
  • The shim line refuses only a %NAME% that cmd.exe would expand, so file URLs with %7E or %20 pass. 2c1b1667 c38d4a2c
  • Double quotes in PATH entries are cut. 0ba37287 cf1abd1b
  • Every child process starts hidden. bb4045e8
  • Host config files with a BOM and CRLF read correctly and keep their layout and line ends on write. cf6a3439 b3a3256d
  • The sandbox shell is Git Bash from the installed Git. 46cb4ca7
  • The current upgrade link is a junction on Windows. 941ea210
  • Kiro, Copilot CLI, VS Code and Codex fixes for Windows homes and paths are in Per agent. 663aa666 5094c639 38c191ee 135120eb
  • Store labels for path sources use forward slashes on every OS. bbee5c28
  • Doctor prints the report path with forward slashes. 6598e099
  • Hook start loads node:fs through getBuiltinModule, cutting Windows hook overhead (perf). 12a51f7c

</details>

Store

  • closeDb checkpoints in PASSIVE mode and skips the checkpoint on a read-only handle, so ctx_stats and ctx_search stay fast. 45145b88 086bde8b
  • Writable connections run synchronous=NORMAL, so a COMMIT needs no fsync (perf). fbb923d9
  • A reserved source row goes away when its write fails; the retention cap counts real bytes. e2a822a6 c56e86a9
  • A project dir outside any git repo is a project keyed by its realpath. d32aa0cf
  • rankTexts runs on a one-column contentless FTS5 table with the same BM25 scores (perf). 82655e34

Performance

  • The hook bundle is minified: 155 KB to 91 KB parse per call. 92f165e7
  • Hook start skips fs/promises, readline, streams and node:crypto, and loads child_process and randomUUID only when needed. b227991d 4bbccb42 639da517
  • The hook reads stdin and writes stdout with sync fd I/O. 6d56a48c 43d1e806
  • resolveProject computes the project key, dir and db path on first read. 7d75314c
  • PostToolUse chunks output once and selects in memory for both sizes. dd2be182
  • See also the perf lines in Search, Sandbox, Windows and Store.

heygen-com/hyperframes ↗ 4 releases · 4 stable
v0.9.16 Stable2026-11-05T13:31:59Z

HyperFrames v0.9.16

Released on 2026-11-05.

Capture now retries a refused screenshot once before reporting it, and Studio's cancel route replies only after the agent has stopped. Windows init no longer crashes at a drive root, and checks skip contrast samples taken mid-motion.

Features

  • Studio: Alt marquee removes the covered items from the selection (bdf9ba0ee, #5680).
  • CLI: Print the render's reference_background as an @hf-result line and in batch manifest rows (58b9ec0ef, #7203).

Fixes

  • CLI: Billing re-reads the account on Enter and sells nothing it can't price (5f7b85c5d, #7226).
  • Engine: Read a video's length from its packets when headers state none (2af37ac0c, #7233).
  • CLI: Every settings file follows HYPERFRAMES_CONFIG_DIR (216395e98, #7229).
  • Core: Keep long WebKit sources and unloaded clips on the audio clock (b7a8cfa73, #7225).
  • CLI: Previews and every per-user cache follow HYPERFRAMES_CACHE_DIR (ecf0e1438, #7224).
  • Producer: Resolve template sub-composition entries in the shared compile stage (0180c603d, #7217).
  • Core: A multi-worker render replays overlapping tweens behind the timeline stand-in (b96d3c8bd, #7231).
  • Producer: Export sizes an unsized phone video as it plays, not as stored (659ef568d, #7228).
  • CLI: Capture takes a refused screenshot once more before naming it (ada71da92, #7222).
  • Skills: Reword the billing lines the Snyk audit reads as hiding cost (7bebcb9fc, #7218).
  • Studio Server: The cancel route replies once the adapter's stop has settled (18513d46d, #7221).
  • Engine: Report a pending setup as a setup timeout, not zero duration (b98718fe9, #7211).
  • Studio Server: A refused cut says which way it was refused (7050b9b5b, #7212).
  • CLI: Init no longer crashes copying into a folder at a Windows drive root (d810bdbb2, #7220).
  • Core: Blur scan and eventful reads put back the DOM a timeline call changed (898d0fb00, #7191).
  • CLI: Billing stops checkout work once the payment wait ends, even mid sign-in refresh (060e6e4a1, #7216).
  • CLI: Two CLI fixes from Wenbo's reverse test (paywall link, plan label) (1fecf5b82, #7099).
  • CLI: Reverse start makes its --dir project, and init takes the video already in it (6728b82e6, #7089).
  • CLI: Measure reads an odd-sized box exactly (823cd126e, #7090).
  • CLI: Timeline apply checks the overlap of a plan's end state, not each step (fa1942d5a, #7214).
  • Core: A scrolled list keeps its offset when blur moves the root (aa34cffe4, #7190).
  • Lint: A video gets no timing from its wrapper, and the docs and hints stop implying it (e976fdbfa, #7210).
  • CLI: Check skips a contrast sample measured over a layer that is mid-motion (7862e271d, #7205).
  • Core: Stop script loading time from shifting WAAPI frames (982070388, #5017).
  • CLI: Bound the loopback Studio probe so a dropped connection costs 1 s, not 10 (6e8454d6d, #6466).
  • CLI: Lint and check accept --lint-verbose, and two skills stop teaching --describe false (924bb64da, #7201).
  • Runtime: Fail readiness waits when a buildReady promise rejects (071ec1fdd, #5746).

Docs & Examples

  • Download links name where the visit began (4ba47aaa1, #7197).

Catalog

  • Registry: Add path-traced-3d example with raster preview and path-traced export (d452a6fad, #7219).

Internal

  • Engine: Pin the motion blur sweep defects A to O by their symptoms (f3e5b81df, #7149).
  • Codegen: Resync cloud client from EF dd7ca48f (3b3d7c2cd, #7207).

Full changelog

https://github.com/heygen-com/hyperframes/compare/v0.9.15...v0.9.16

v0.9.15 Stable2026-11-05T02:36:54Z

HyperFrames v0.9.15

Released on 2026-11-05.

Local renders on FFmpeg 4 no longer turn a finished mix down by several dB when they match its length to the video. Motion blur can now choose when its shutter opens.

Features

  • Core: data-motion-blur-phase sets when the motion-blur shutter opens in each frame (aace91b, #6983).

Fixes

  • Render: On FFmpeg 4, a mix already under the peak limit is no longer turned down by several dB when its length is matched to the video. Two near-identical mixes no longer land about 4 LU apart (f6ae9bf, #7199).

v0.9.14 Stable2026-11-05T01:33:00Z

HyperFrames v0.9.14

Released on 2026-11-04.

Cloud renders no longer fail on common font styles, and they now carry the system fonts a project uses. A wedged render stops itself, and check no longer hangs on long videos.

Features

  • CLI: Cloud render carries the system fonts a project uses in its zip (cba66f20a69c9, #7165).

Fixes

  • CLI: Tell a failed billing checkout apart from a payment not finished in time (60ca970ed60df, #7185).
  • Fonts: Stop failing cloud renders on var() shorthands, font-X-400 names and linked font sheets (af0c815c72b14, #7166).
  • CLI: Check copies only the stretch of a video it plays and stops after 2 minutes of copies (c6f87f59b0c9a, #7182).
  • CLI: Report why a reverse session could not be recorded (21e2038595047, #7186).
  • CLI: Check thins its craft samples when frames are slow, so the pass ends near 90 seconds (be8122d0c4054, #7183).
  • CLI: A command waiting on the project lock says what it waits for (00ea86570a8c8, #7184).
  • Engine: A background image that never finishes decoding no longer stalls the frame (73e65e2c7826b, #6972).
  • CLI: A track with no beats answers an empty result, not an error (cffd70f2f9b24, #7173).
  • Core: A logo image no longer renders stretched to the frame bottom (3d78ac0fa6671, #7179).
  • CLI: A wedged render trips its 60 s watchdog, one local render at a time, and fonts the cloud can't resolve are flagged (d4e4b07e3cbc1, #7168).

Docs & Examples

  • Nav: The docs menu button names the section you are in (054cf4f119a76, #7133).
  • Drop the hidden add-a-transition button; hand-off now works on windows and linux (97b805dcbdb11, #7132).
  • Say HyperFrames is free, and what the Studio app's Pro plan adds (c3eb052545668, #7131).

Catalog

  • Catalog: A search box over the cards; it fills the ?q= filter the gallery already reads (b2b034fcd88f9, #7147).

Internal

  • Studio: The studio-open journey counts the same thumbnails on every runner image (2f321e0b0bb13, #7187).
  • Studio: The timeline viewport gate runs one pinned Chrome build, not the runner's (2679bcdee71aa, #7180).

Full changelog

https://github.com/heygen-com/hyperframes/compare/v0.9.13...v0.9.14

v0.9.13 Stable2026-11-04T18:50:33Z

HyperFrames v0.9.13

Released on 2026-11-04.

A render that stalls while its pages load now retries with fewer workers instead of hanging. A full disk now says so, and splitting a clip that follows another clip cuts where it plays.

Fixes

  • Producer: Retry with fewer workers when every page times out loading (04bc4737ad1d3, #7171).
  • Lint: Flag a data-automation or data-fx-chain the render cannot read (cd7b7383cb19c, #7169).
  • Studio Server: Splitting a clip that starts after another clip cuts where it plays (0bbc9b2943ad6, #7172).
  • Producer: A drive that refuses frame links gets a copy, whatever code it refuses with (845bd638a7784, #7163).
  • Engine: A full disk during frame extraction says so, not that the video is broken (ac1948a88b89a, #7161).
  • CLI: Address skills install and update hints to the person (604a25fbaaba5, #7160).
  • CLI: A failed run exits 1 when its output pipe closes early (b3b6a8116bb58, #7158).

Docs & Examples

  • Discord in the top bar and footer, and each Discord link tagged by where it sits (21f2119194ffd, #7153).

Tencent/BrowserSkill ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
cli-v0.4.2bsk CLI 0.4.2 Stable2026-11-05T12:24:29Z

BrowserSkill CLI 0.4.2 adds MCP access and more reliable updates, cancellation, and action outcomes. Use it with extension 0.4.2 for the browser-side fixes below.

Added

  • MCP server: bsk mcp exposes BrowserSkill over stdio through the existing daemon and extension. bsk install-skill --mcp configures Codex, Claude Code, or Cursor. The initial adapter covers its tool schemas; file transfers, recording, and full-page screenshot export remain available through the CLI or DSH plugin.
  • Controller identity: session startup accepts an agent name, project, and color, shown in the extension's shared-control overlay and session metadata.
  • Date and time inputs: fill supports date, time, datetime-local, month, and week, with format and min/max validation.

Changed

  • CLI and daemon updates prefer the official website's domestic download source and fall back to GitHub on download or verification failures. Archive fallback stays on the selected version and retains SHA256 verification. Explicit manifest overrides continue to use only the configured source.
  • Borrowed tabs return relative to their original neighbors, with improved pinned-tab and group restoration. Eligible requests can automatically borrow a tab when borrow confirmation is disabled.
  • Shared control now waits for two seconds of quiet after the person's last completed action, up from 650 ms.

Fixed

  • Timed-out page actions cancel and await bounded cleanup. Errors preserve execution phase, effect state, and operation identity. Missing receipts remain explicitly uncertain; CLI and MCP guidance asks callers to inspect the application result before choosing another action.
  • Cancellation arriving before daemon RPC registration is retried within a shared two-second confirmation/response budget, reducing screenshots that keep a session busy after the caller stops.
  • Windows remote pairing, credential rotation, and revocation retry authorization snapshot replacement when concurrent readers cause Access is denied.
  • Shared-control sync recovers missing frame collectors, reports failed child frames safely, preserves human holds, and retries transient coverage failures within its deadline.
  • Recovery guidance identifies the sync reply's top-level observation_id. Borrow guidance explains closed tabs and unavailable confirmation UI.
  • DSH fill/select values beginning with - are passed as values instead of being parsed as CLI options.

Coordinated extension fixes

  • Restore legacy mousewheel handlers, including Bilibili player volume controls, even when no BrowserSkill task is active.
  • Avoid repeated page reloads caused by enabling console capture before it is requested.
  • Prevent truncated emoji text from invalidating an entire daemon reply and leaving commands waiting until timeout.
  • Let automated clicks reach page controls beneath the shared-control toolbar; preserve successful Enter-triggered navigation receipts.
  • Keep scrolling, ineffective keys, and focus alone from unnecessarily taking control; release completed/cancelled gestures and composition holds correctly.
  • Preserve main-document target actions across unrelated iframe refreshes, recheck selectors after node replacement, and associate repeated action labels with their own DOM containers.
  • Verify actual window and viewport sizes after resizing, and refresh shared-control observations.
  • Extend sensitive-field redaction across shared-control state, expectation failures, extraction, recording, and saved HTML debug evidence; recognize compound autocomplete tokens and keep recognized password fields masked after reveal. Redact vendor credential headers in debug evidence.

Upgrade

  1. Finish active browser tasks and run bsk update --yes. Restart foreground or supervisor-managed daemons in their owning terminal or process manager; after replacing the executable with an installer, run bsk daemon restart.
  2. Install extension 0.4.2 and update the DSH plugin to 0.4.2. Store review may delay availability.
  3. Reload the browser-skill instructions. DSH users should restart the profile and invoke /browser-skill again. Restart MCP clients after adding their configuration.
  4. Check bsk --version, bsk status, and bsk doctor.

ext-v0.4.2BrowserSkill Extension 0.4.2 Stable2026-11-05T12:13:48Z

BrowserSkill Extension 0.4.2 fixes everyday page interaction regressions and improves shared-control reliability, action outcomes, and sensitive-data protection. Pair it with CLI/daemon 0.4.2.

Added

  • Controller identity: browser overlays display the agent name, project, session ID, and color supplied by the CLI or DSH client.
  • Date and time inputs: fill native date, time, datetime-local, month, and week fields, including background-tab handling and format/min/max validation.

Changed

  • Scrolling, ineffective keys, and focusing a text field alone no longer unnecessarily take control from the Agent. Real clicks, keyboard activation, and actual edits still yield to the person. Pending gestures pause dispatch without revoking the current context; coordinate input waits for scrolling to settle.
  • Shared control waits for two seconds of quiet after the person's last completed action, up from 650 ms. Completed/cancelled gestures, input-method composition, and native select interactions release their holds correctly.
  • Borrowed tabs return relative to their original neighbors, with improved pinned-tab and group restoration. Eligible requests support automatic borrowing when borrow confirmation is disabled.
  • The extension restores the tabGroups permission to read and restore borrowed tabs' original groups.

Fixed

  • Broken wheel interactions: restore legacy mousewheel handlers, such as Bilibili's player volume control, which could stop working even without an active BrowserSkill task.
  • Repeated page reloads: enable console capture only when requested, avoiding anti-debugging reload loops on affected sites.
  • Unexpected command timeouts: replace lone Unicode surrogates left by truncated emoji so the daemon no longer discards the entire reply.
  • Toolbar-blocked clicks: the shared-control toolbar and its children yield pointer hits during automated clicks, including page actions beneath the lower-right corner, then become interactive again.
  • Enter navigation receipts: dispatch Enter keydown and text together, retain completed link/form navigation outcomes, and avoid sending a separate character event into the new document.
  • Timeout cleanup and diagnostics: cancelled actions retain execution phase and effect-state information; missing completion receipts remain explicitly uncertain.
  • Repeated action labels: use each action's own DOM container for context, including labels after the button, instead of mixing text from adjacent records or other frames.
  • Dynamic element waits: requery selectors when matched nodes are replaced, avoiding false hidden/detached success while a replacement remains visible.
  • Unrelated iframe refreshes: keep main-document target actions valid when a carousel, video, or ad iframe refreshes. Frame-targeted and strict-observation actions still require a fresh sync after document changes.
  • Frame synchronization: repair missing collectors, report failed frames safely, preserve human holds, and bound retries for temporary coverage failures.
  • Window resizing: verify resulting bounds and the target tab's viewport rather than echoing the requested size, and refresh subsequent observations.
  • Sensitive fields: extend redaction for passwords, one-time codes, and payment-card fields across shared-control state, expectation failures, extraction, recording, and saved HTML debug evidence. Match compound autocomplete tokens, preserve masking for recognized password fields after reveal, and redact vendor credential headers.

Upgrade

  1. Finish active browser tasks and update the CLI/daemon to 0.4.2.
  2. Install extension 0.4.2 from the store or the ZIP attached to this release. Store submissions require review; check the installed version.
  3. Update the DSH plugin to 0.4.2 and reload browser-skill instructions. Check bsk status and bsk doctor.

CLI 0.4.2 also adds bsk mcp for MCP clients and domestic update downloads with GitHub fallback. See the CLI release for setup and daemon-side changes.

stablyai/orca ↗ 3 releases · 2 stable · 1 prerelease
v1.4.250 Stable2026-11-05T10:40:31Z

We did two releases today! Be sure to check out the v1.4.249 release notes, which include easier Chat or Terminal setup, more control over conversations, and a redesigned phone Home.

Lower terminal memory use, clearer chat changes & a Chrome-compatible password manager ✨

  • ⚡ Lower terminal memory use: Stop repeatedly reading and parsing font files for terminal ligatures, fixing a source of high memory use and launch crashes in sessions with many terminals.
  • ⚡ Faster worktree switching: Performance improvements make switching between worktrees faster.
  • 📝 See what agents changed: Chat turns show changed files and line counts, with patches available inline on desktop and phone.
  • 🔐 Chrome-compatible password manager: Enable in Settings → Browser to save, fill, import Chrome passwords, and manage logins, encrypted locally with your OS’s secure storage.
  • 💬 Chat UI keeps up with your agent: Long, tool-heavy turns stay connected in Chat UI, and new chats follow your agent’s own model configuration unless you choose a model.
  • 🖥️ More reliable terminals and hosts: Prevent stray escape-sequence text after terminal output gaps, and make paired-server workers and usage polling available on headless hosts.
  • 📱 Better phone continuity: Send chat with Cmd/Ctrl+Enter and keep paired devices following the tabs they open.

Highlights

  • Performance: Worktree switching is faster. Terminal ligatures no longer parse an entire font for every terminal. In the PR’s Windows test with Commit Mono, 32 terminals relaunched without the previous out-of-memory crash; results depend on fonts and workload. Closing a tool in a long agent turn also avoids scanning every other live tool.
  • Chat: Successful Claude and Codex edits appear as changed-file summaries in the conversation, with patch details loaded on selection. Chat UI no longer interrupts an otherwise healthy agent turn when it exceeds 128 live text or tool entries. Codex chats show requested wait durations and support goal pause, resume and clear.
  • Browser: Save and fill logins in Orca’s browser and manage them in browser settings. Passwords use OS-backed encryption; the vault is unavailable when secure storage is unavailable.
  • Terminal & remote hosts: Terminal stream recovery avoids printing partial escape sequences after output gaps. Headless hosts start the services needed for paired-server workers and usage polling.
  • Workspace & onboarding: Choose Chat UI during onboarding, hide the onboarding checklist, find hidden discovered worktrees outside project grouping, and use the improved inactive workspace cleanup interface.

Compatibility & upgrade notes

  • Terminal ligatures: Common ligatures remain available. Font-specific ligatures outside the built-in list may no longer join with the GPU renderer.
  • Browser passwords: The vault is available in the desktop browser, including local and SSH workspace pages; streamed paired-server pages and the web client do not have a vault. Secure OS encryption is required.
  • Chat model defaults: A new chat without an explicit model choice uses the agent’s configuration. The displayed default can be stale until the agent starts and reports its model.
  • Terminal recovery: Remote-runtime streams retain their existing recovery behavior.

v1.4.249 Stable2026-11-04T23:53:56Z

Easier setup, more control over chats & tidier workspaces ✨

  • 🎛️ Choose Chat or Terminal from the start: Onboarding now lets you pick Chat UI or Terminal, then choose an agent and install the skills for that setup.
  • 💬 Keep control of conversations: Preserve chat inputs through uncertain delivery and Stop, choose whether sending moves the scroll position, and decide when idle chat agents stop—or keep them running.
  • 🗂️ Keep projects tidy: Adding a project keeps discovered worktrees hidden, while create and clone handle symlinked parent folders consistently.
  • 📝 Make reading and editing your own: Set the default Markdown view, optionally insert a final newline on save, and adjust chat width from pane headers.
  • 📱 A clearer phone Home: The updated phone app separates Feed, Usage, Automations and Settings, shows more agent usage, and names recent conversations.
  • 🧩 Small fixes across daily work: Keep terminal scroll position on input, open file links in their workspace, render Mermaid in PR comments, and close floating browser splits from their own toolbar.

Highlights

  • Setup & skills: Pick Chat or Terminal during onboarding, use the updated agent picker and theme step, and install Chat skills for the selected provider on this computer. Empty newly added projects can open the chosen Chat agent when that route is supported.
  • Chat behavior: Inputs and queued follow-ups are retained through uncertain delivery and Stop. New settings control scroll position when sending and when idle chat agents stop. Loaded chat images stay visible across scrolling and workspace switches, and Pi compaction holds sends until it finishes.
  • Agent choices: Managed Claude accounts keep later /model and effort choices when the defaults have not changed. Claude fallback is shown without changing saved model choices; OpenCode effort follows the selected model, and Claude chat adds Ultracode effort.
  • Projects & navigation: Adding a project keeps discovered worktrees hidden. Project creation and cloning resolve symlinked parents consistently; manual workspace order and type-to-search in the new-workspace agent picker receive fixes.
  • Editor & browser: Choose the default Markdown view, enable Insert Final Newline on save, preserve chat HTML appearance in file previews, and close a floating browser split without closing the terminal beside it.
  • Phone: The updated app adds Home tabs, usage for more agents and conversation names in Jump back in. It also improves terminal input, effort controls, HTML previews and Android theme/navigation behavior.
  • Automation & notifications: Automations use the shared agent picker, accept Pi model/thinking arguments and refresh when projects change. Interrupted terminal automation runs are recorded as failed, acknowledged desktop alerts clear, and OpenCode completion notifications handle OSC 7501.

Compatibility & upgrade notes

  • Existing setup choices: The new onboarding choices use existing surface and agent preferences. This release does not reset everyone's default agent or switch every existing workspace to Chat. A workspace with existing tabs keeps them; the new-project Chat path depends on a supported runtime and agent.
  • Chat settings: Scroll-on-send and idle-agent stopping are configurable. Choose Never if you want idle chat agents to remain running; stopping an idle agent is separate from deleting the conversation.
  • Managed Claude accounts: Unchanged defaults stop overwriting later account choices after settings synchronization. This does not recover choices overwritten by an older build, and changed default keys still apply.
  • Paired servers: Improvements to paired-server file links use the new capability when available; older servers retain their existing path. There is no new minimum server version for the setup changes. GitHub issue limitations with older servers remain below.
  • Phone changes: Native phone UI and Android fixes require an updated Orca Mobile app as well as a compatible desktop or server. The mobile changes are included in Orca Mobile 0.0.64; updating desktop alone does not install a native phone release.
  • Orchestration panel: The experimental panel and its setting are temporarily disabled again. Orchestration messaging changes are separate from availability of that panel.

Known issues

Chat setup with no default agent: With Chat UI enabled and the default agent cleared in Settings, Setup Guide's Install skills button can remain disabled without an explanation. Choose a default agent, such as Claude, before installing.

Shell history after a cold restart: Some Mac shell panes can reopen without showing their previous output after a full app and terminal restart. This class of failure was also observed on v1.4.248; the cause remains unresolved. This release does not claim to fix it.

GitHub issues on older paired servers: Opening an issue through a repository or URL can leave title, label and assignee editing unavailable. Phone issue Close/Reopen can also refuse an older server or intermediary. Open on GitHub remains available.

Saved chat storage & performance: Live-history compaction remains paused, so storage can grow as conversations continue. This release does not claim to remove every source of chat stalls. Hook changes reduce subprocess work, but whole-app latency, CPU and heap improvements were not measured.

Long update delays: With a 14- or 30-day delay, a release can leave the update feed before it becomes eligible. None and Skip Delay Once remain available.

Plugin command replacements: A plugin replacing a built-in command can still finish after Orca has used the normal command instead, causing the action to happen twice.

openclaw/openclaw ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v2026.11.1openclaw 2026.11.1 Stable2026-11-05T09:48:37Z

2026.11.1

Highlights

  • Safer Mac settings: failed config saves no longer overwrite secrets, and turning off Tailscale exposure keeps Gateway authentication intact. (#187734, #187664) Fixes #187600, #187597 Thanks @anmol0b.
  • More reliable channel replies: Telegram, Discord, and webhook conversations keep reaching the agent after request handlers or earlier turns finish. (#179200, #179385, #179704, #189457) Fixes #178554 Thanks @steipete, @LiuwqGit, @jalehman, and @vincentkoc.
  • Conversations survive more interruptions: oversized turns retain useful history, and stopping the first Claude CLI turn no longer starts the next message from scratch. (#191146, #181966, #186380) Fixes #191133, #181945 Thanks @Yun-0000, @obviyus, @mirasrael, and @holgergruenhagen.
  • Update recovery gets unstuck after crashed or canceled updaters, restores the Windows Gateway after failed verification, and avoids repeated full-database copying during large-state checks. (#177932, #186787, #189647, #182632, #187921) Fixes #177684, #184893, #189620 Thanks @duyufansh2008, @RomneyDa, @fuller-stack-dev, @TheAngryPit, @sukhdeepjohar, @steipete, @syxscott, and @stephen3303.
  • Subagents keep their follow-ups through model and session changes, while parent sessions receive child approvals even with a custom session store. (#186238, #183501, #189827) Fixes #179289, #189823 Thanks @steipete, @Marvinthebored, @RomneyDa, and @ekinnee.
  • Android End Talk with a compatible Gateway stops listening without canceling accepted agent work, so its answer can still arrive in chat; explicit cancellation remains available. (#172276, #188798) Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • Provider recovery respects provider-specified rate-limit timing, and Gemini Flash text models use Interactions by default on the official API-key endpoint while custom proxies retain their existing default. (#180280, #189395) Thanks Ayaan Zaidi, @evomach, @sukhdeepjohar, @RomneyDa.

Changes

  • Claude CLI: saved subscription accounts can make configured models available without a separate native login, and operators can opt into native Agent and Workflow orchestration through plugins.entries.anthropic.config.managedNativeTools; the default remains off and restricted runs keep their existing tool limits. (#179203, #183765) Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • Anthropic: API-key setup can record an optional Anthropic workspace ID; existing workspace headers are preserved, and workspace-scoped keys need no extra setting. (#186976) Thanks Ayaan Zaidi, @evomach, @sukhdeepjohar, @RomneyDa.
  • Google: Interactions requests honor managed proxy and TLS settings, rotate eligible API keys before streaming after a rate limit, preserve native video input, and forward nucleus sampling consistently; choose google-generative-ai when explicit Gemini cache handles are required. (#189395, #190695) Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • Bedrock Mantle: discovered numbered OpenAI GPT models select the Responses route automatically, while GPT-OSS keeps Chat Completions and Claude keeps Anthropic Messages. Thanks @MertBasar0, @anmol0b, @shakkernerd, @kybrcore, @sukhdeepjohar, @potoior, @RomneyDa.
  • Control UI: the model picker shows the configured active context budget rather than only the model maximum, and short session links retain their intended navigation target. (#180870, #183148) Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • Google Chat: remote HTTP(S) attachments are delivered as visible links alongside captions; local files and native uploads remain unsupported with service-account authentication. Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • Update diagnostics: detailed readiness separates a ready core from channel outages, allowing repaired updaters to finish with named channel warnings without hiding HTTP 503 or weakening normal service-start checks. Thanks @duyufansh2008, @RomneyDa.

Fixes

  • Network and tool safety: ClickClack and ClawHub reject redirects into unrelated private services, Discord CDN downloads no longer inherit browser private-network access, and FIFO native-hook credentials are rejected without hanging the agent. (#190944, #190939, #191458, #191423) Thanks @pgondhi987, @SebTardif, and @Leon-SK668.
  • Doctor consent and access policy: noninteractive checks no longer download plugins without repair consent, and explicitly empty group allowlists remain empty instead of being repopulated. (#191523, #174787) Fixes #191420, #173826 Thanks @brandonulrich, @steipete, and @lockhartheavyindustries.
  • Recovery data: malformed UTF-8 legacy imports are rejected instead of replacing original content; failed skill restore rollbacks preserve the original copy, and cleanup refuses destructive work when workspace inspection fails. (#180861, #173954, #174195, #176362) Fixes #180782, #180795 Thanks @RomneyDa, @duyufansh2008, @steipete, and @wangmiao0668000666.
  • Log redaction: malformed custom entries no longer disable valid rules or built-in secret protection; invalid policy is reported while safe redaction remains active. (#173471) Thanks @skyzhao1223 and @RomneyDa.
  • Secret egress: reject chains relying on the distrusted emSign ECC G3 root in the managed proxy and exported subprocess CA bundle; restart the Gateway after updating, and update clients with independent trust stores separately. (#179249) Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • macOS connectivity: configured credentials take precedence over stale app environment, typed environment secret references resolve correctly, and remote Settings respects the Control UI base path. (#191468, #185141, #128175) Fixes #164596 Thanks @RomneyDa, @anmol0b, and @goldmar.
  • Mac tools and messaging: iMessage remains usable after app updates without re-granting Automation, Peekaboo window inspection accepts valid snapshot references, and node exec preserves results for commands configured without a timeout. (#186107, #172116, #190729) Fixes #178719 Thanks @aniketkrs, @RomneyDa, @anmol0b, @SharkeyDev, @holgergruenhagen, @LiuwqGit, and @etzelm.
  • Mac voice and embedded pages: avoid duplicate voice sends and false sent feedback, and show a recoverable load error instead of a blank or stale Mac tab. (#186271, #185432) Thanks @anmol0b, @etzelm, and @RomneyDa.
  • Native voice and capture: iOS and macOS Talk tolerate slow audio acknowledgments within bounded buffering, long meeting replies are not cut off merely because generation outruns playback, and oversized Mac recordings fail with size guidance instead of dropping the node connection. Thanks @MertBasar0, @anmol0b, @shakkernerd, @kybrcore, @sukhdeepjohar, @potoior, and @RomneyDa.
  • Android and desktop compatibility: Android Chat waits for the role-allowed agent list after switching Gateways, and independently updated Mac nodes can return optional cursor coordinates without a computer-use contract error. Thanks @MertBasar0, @anmol0b, @shakkernerd, @kybrcore, @sukhdeepjohar, @potoior, @RomneyDa.
  • Channel recovery: Slack detects dead relay connections without treating ordinary silence as failure, Tlon reconnects stalled streams so unacknowledged events can replay, and managed Tailscale exposure recovers after a daemon restart while local authenticated access stays available. Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • Channel delivery: Feishu keeps text from before and after tool calls in the same streaming card; Synology confirms delivery only from recipient-matched message IDs; Twitch targets and Zalo unsupported-event text follow their channel handling rather than silently losing usable content. Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • Channel credentials and notices: implicit Slack default accounts retain complete top-level credentials alongside named accounts, channel secret consumers respect account activation, and Nostr reports failed pairing notifications without undoing approval. Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • iMessage storage: recovery cursor keys no longer contain a PostgreSQL-incompatible NUL separator, allowing the channel to start with PostgreSQL-backed state. (#184919) Fixes #184862 Thanks @duyufansh2008, @RomneyDa, and @etzelm.
  • Session and agent authority: deleting one agent no longer breaks other agents' tool authority, and local session-capability changes stop inappropriate provider fallback instead of trying every model. (#174918, #179312) Fixes #179290 Thanks @steipete, @chelsealong, @obviyus, and @Marvinthebored.
  • Prompt caching: session tool definitions stay stable across turns, including completions, heartbeats, and non-owner requests, avoiding unnecessary prompt-cache misses. (#175309, #176167) Thanks @obviyus.
  • Conversation recovery: compaction retains the active human request across repeated summaries; queue overflow summaries retain each visible sender and conversation context; exec completion guidance survives overflow. (#180214, #187833, #190698, #182188, #184216) Thanks @RomneyDa and @aniketkrs.
  • Codex continuity: conversation-wide restrictions retain compatible native threads, temporary restrictions preserve the ordinary conversation binding, and a missing thread with recorded history reports an error instead of silently starting over. Thanks @skyzhao1223 and @RomneyDa.
  • Codex replies: transient retries can summarize the original request and completed tools without rerunning them; terminal handoffs allow already-submitted steering a bounded chance to confirm consumption and do not automatically replay uncertain input. (#183077, #184062, #181839, #167390) Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • Shared credentials and model availability: concurrent MCP callers can reuse a fresh OAuth token without unnecessary exclusive locking, rate-limit cooldowns follow provider timing instead of accumulating hours of local backoff, and fresh model publications replace superseded refresh results. (#179997, #180280, #179144) Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • Claude CLI and scheduled work: canceled cron runs stop reply hooks, interrupted long-running jobs no longer prevent scheduler startup, oversized or invalid CLI streams cancel their subprocess promptly, and managed completions retain the policy-filtered tool catalog. (#181245, #191422, #159931, #172026) Thanks @steipete and @fuller-stack-dev.
  • Session history and memory: long transcript decoding releases database read snapshots promptly, bounded history pages keep their requested anchors and adjacent messages, and memory indexing waits for archived transcript materialization. (#164442, #172970, #177937, #182091, #190182, #189284, #188450, #188483, #190690) Fixes #171467 Thanks @RomneyDa, and @etzelm.
  • Subagent and restart recovery: live descendants keep their expired parents available for communication, accepted yields settle sibling tool results before handoff, and stale interrupted input no longer blocks a new human message indefinitely. (#179057, #184021, #180853, #181871, #178888, #184041, #184069, #172959) Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • Agent handoffs: waiting session sends recover the stored long reply instead of returning only a short lifecycle preview, and ACP-created sessions retain the requested working directory for later prompts. (#178271, #176852) Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • Control UI recovery: silently dead Gateway connections reconnect without replaying uncertain sends, consumed slash-steering messages leave the queue, and routine restart notices stay concise. (#179101, #178642, #180024, #172843) Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • GitHub workflows: active sessions can open the reconnect flow, failed follow-up pushes retain the existing PR, and readers no longer hang while discarding response bodies. (#180200, #187835, #191721) Fixes #187832 Thanks @RomneyDa, @anmol0b, @obviyus, and @nocodet888-arch.
  • Plugin lifecycle: startup avoids recopying managed binaries, repairs owned stale host links after installation-root changes, and keeps plugin background readers alive for admitted callbacks and cleanup; internal plugin reload resumes stay in the current session rather than using an old external recipient. (#187865, #181140, #182207, #179031, #181270, #179549, #190982) Thanks @Satoshi-Kusumoto, @RomneyDa, @jasonftl, and @obviyus.
  • Gateway startup and shutdown: retry transient database locks during stale-claim cleanup and sidecar startup, and exit after clean shutdown instead of waiting for systemd to kill a process with a leaked handle. (#187724, #186920, #191251) Fixes #187723, #186875, #191119 Thanks @masatohoshino, @obviyus, @steipete, @yousan, @bianyuxia-commits, and @davesanvil.
  • Update startup and migration: recover with retained worker indexes, avoid crashes in automatic migration recovery from 2026.10.7–2026.10.9, preserve recovery errors, and reject Git targets that require an unavailable newer Node before staging. (#189023, #189713, #191495, #181715) Thanks @RomneyDa and @steipete.
  • Update repair: release abandoned cancellation custody and resolve pending package steps without repair loops; restore a suspended Windows Gateway task after failed verification while retaining the original failure details. (#177932, #186787, #189647) Fixes #177684, #184893, #189620 Thanks @RomneyDa, @fuller-stack-dev, @TheAngryPit, @sukhdeepjohar, @steipete, @syxscott, and @stephen3303.
  • Update captures and service handoff: ignore Finder metadata when listing recovery captures, recover CLI Node runtimes when launched from /, and classify an unavailable automatic-update service handoff as skipped rather than an unrecorded failure. (#186663, #178112, #188405) Fixes #186650, #177739, #188302 Thanks @duyufansh2008, @RomneyDa, @steipete, @woshicby, @downingkeys, and @ARSA31.
  • Doctor migrations: bound long Matrix archive filenames, preserve config promotion when only migration guidance changes, run explicitly requested headless repairs, return failure rather than success when required work was skipped, and retain invalid heartbeat quiet hours with actionable validation. (#187443, #190665, #187192, #186305) Fixes #187390, #190658, #187122 Thanks @RomneyDa, @skyzhao1223, @steipete, @dakoller, @saariuslystoned, and @JordanNewell.
  • Configuration and store recovery: explain how to recover when an older binary meets newer config; relative session stores resolve against the active state directory, and Doctor merges legacy misplaced stores with backups while preserving conflicting conversations and refusing unsafe retirement. (#187639, #182444, #181554, #180414, #179089) Fixes #152791 Thanks @RomneyDa, @duyufansh2008, @steipete, and @kip-claw.
  • Service and backup reliability: repaired updaters distinguish durable database changes from read-only WAL bookkeeping, wait for the serving Mac child to release state, preserve valid replacement config without metadata, and keep unrelated service overrides intact during supported Node repairs. Thanks @RomneyDa.
  • Diagnostics and provider tools: timeout messages distinguish an unknown tool outcome from a safe retry, Anthropic PDF requests use the selected account's proper authentication, and Gemini/Vertex streams stop at terminal markers instead of consuming later frames. (#180247, #182429, #184434, #178420, #117397) Thanks Ayaan Zaidi, @evomach, @sukhdeepjohar, @RomneyDa, @MertBasar0, @anmol0b, @shakkernerd, @kybrcore, @potoior.

career-ops-v1.39.0career-ops v1.39.0: After the Scan Stable2026-11-05T08:55:09Z

<!-- co:release-notes:career-ops-v1.39.0 --> This release is about the steps after the scan: reading an interview invite, keeping your CV in your own words and checking a UK visa sponsor before you apply. It comes from 99 people over two weeks, 69 of them contributing for the first time.

Highlights

  • Interview invites, read for you. Paste an invite and invite-match.mjs lists what it asks of you (no AI, a recording, camera on, ID, a panel) (#5734) and drafts a short clarification email that you send yourself (#6100). panelist-roles.mjs classifies the people on the panel by role (#5799), and what an interviewer said they care about carries into your plan for the next round (#5801). All by @Schlaflied.
  • Your CV, in your words. PDF and LaTeX output stop when generated text adds a stock resume phrase or a dash that your own CV does not use. Lines you wrote stay as they are. (#6178 by @mbrow190)
  • Less of you in the prompt. With privacy.redact_contact_info: true in config/profile.yml, the Gemini, OpenAI and OpenRouter evaluator scripts take your name, email, phone and profile links out of the prompt (#2427 by @GambitATJ). Values you list in config/privacy.yml stop a request from the standalone evaluator scripts before it is sent (#5571 by @asimthaha). Both are opt-in, and neither changes what your coding agent sends.
  • A UK visa sponsor check. node sponsor-check.mjs --company "<name>" looks the employer up in the Home Office register of licensed sponsors, and --tracker checks the employers in your tracker. It only reads. (#5790 by @foayenix)
  • Sixteen more places to look. Apple (#5496 by @kellymmcgrath), ApplicantPro (#6252 by @Schlaflied), Bulldogjob (#6253 by @momoscape), ClearCompany, Homerun and WP Job Manager boards (#5918, #5785, #5878 by @benbalter), Darwinbox (#6416 by @okeken), Deel (#6189 by @dgitman), Factorial (#6394 by @artemtrofymenko), GermanTechJobs (#6346 by @faramirezs), jobs.cz and prace.cz (#5631 by @kosinal), Keka (#6199 by @darkpandawarrior), Notion (#5616 by @IaroslavMazur), Paylocity (#5687 by @ilyakanevskiy) and Zucchetti InRecruiting (#5984 by @Fede046).

If you run scan from a script

  • A misspelled top-level key in portals.yml now stops scan.mjs with exit code 1 and names the file, the key and the likely spelling. Before, the scan found nothing and exited 0. (#6107 by @mbrow190)
  • scan.mjs exits 2 when none of its targets could be read. (#5720 by @vliggio)

Updating

Run node update-system.mjs apply --confirm in your career-ops folder. If you said no to the update prompt earlier, this is the same command.

This release carries 450 pull requests from 99 people, 69 of them contributing for the first time.

different-ai/openwork ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.18.65OpenWork v0.18.65 Stable2026-11-05T07:43:34Z

What's new

OpenWork v0.18.65 desktop release.

  • Public artifacts use the openwork-* naming convention.
  • Cloud artifacts use openwork-cloud-* and require sign-in without enterprise activation.
  • Enterprise artifacts use openwork-enterprise-* while retaining the standard OpenWork app identity.
  • Public, Cloud, and enterprise Electron builds use separate updater manifests on this release.

Windows installers are signed using Microsoft Artifact Signing.

0.11.1-nightly.b3b8ced Prerelease2026-11-05T06:26:46Z

Nightly release from commit: b3b8cede0beb95272ebc2c72c648269f9c5228b3

npm packages, rust crates and python wheels are available under this version 0.11.1-nightly.b3b8ced

Neovim Plugin

  • {target}.so / .dylib / .dll - Lua module for Neovim

C FFI Library (for Bun/Node/Python)

  • c-lib-{target}.so / .dylib / .dll - C FFI library
  • fff-c-{target}.tar.gz (or .zip for Windows) - C SDK bundle (.a static lib, .h header, .pc pkg-config)

MCP Server

  • fff-mcp-{target} - MCP server binary

Python Package

  • python/*.whl / python/*.tar.gz - Python wheels and sdist
  • Install from PyPI: pip install fff-search (when published)

Update mcp via:

curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/dmtrKovalenko/fff/main/install-mcp.sh | bash

rohitg00/agentmemory ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.9.32 Stable2026-11-04T19:06:52Z

Breaking change: an API key alone no longer turns on an outside LLM or embedding provider. Read Breaking before upgrading.

Breaking

  • LLM summaries, compression, consolidation and reflection need AGENTMEMORY_LLM_PROVIDER=anthropic|openai|gemini|openrouter|minimax plus that key; boot, doctor, status and the viewer print the line to add (#1664)
  • Cloud embeddings need EMBEDDING_PROVIDER=<name>; local stays on-device, unset means BM25 and graph only (#1664)
  • OPENAI_API_KEY_FOR_LLM is removed (#1664)
  • Hooks send nothing to a remote plain http:// server; use HTTPS for a remote server (#1664)
  • PreCompact injects stored memory only with AGENTMEMORY_INJECT_CONTEXT=true (#1664)

Added

  • iii installs itself on native Windows, with SHA-256 verification (#1670)
  • Claude Code plugin settings for the server URL and secret; secret kept in the system credential store (#1664)
  • MCP tool annotations (readOnlyHint, destructiveHint) and a privacy policy at https://agent-memory.dev/privacy (#1664)
  • sourceIds on memory_lesson_save (#1661)
  • Official listings: OpenAI plugin directory for Codex (skills, pair with agentmemory connect codex), Hermes plugin catalog (hermes plugins install agentmemory) and ClawHub (openclaw plugins install clawhub:agentmemory)
  • MCP shim crash log mcp-crash.log, saved store before exit (#1662, #1674)
  • Docker Hub image ghumarerohit/agentmemory:<version> and latest for linux/amd64 and linux/arm64, published on each release (#1677)

Fixed

  • Copilot CLI subagent reports keep their reply and are stored once (#1599)
  • Worker Compose starts on native Windows; stop waits for every worker (#1641)
  • No hidden install prompt or hang on a slow iii --version probe on Windows (#1658)
  • Boot waits for the state worker, so slow starts keep the keyword index and vectors (#1627, #1626, #1660)
  • Stale compose PID after a reboot no longer crash-loops (#1622)
  • Docker image keeps local embedding dependencies (#1651)
  • doctor never prompts without a terminal or with CI set (#1671); no SDK debug lines in status/stop (#1672)
  • Slot tools hidden and guarded when AGENTMEMORY_SLOTS is off (#1659)
  • CJK recall (#1553), scoped smart-search ranking (#1669), project isolation (#1647), memory types in search responses (#1646)
  • OpenAI reply cut at max_tokens fails over instead of being stored (#1495)
  • Orphaned index generations reclaimed (#1256); bounded MCP graph stats (#1260)
  • Facet cleanup on deletes (#1642), session sweep (#1645), surrogate-safe hook truncation (#1598), Hermes git probe stdin (#1663), Cline MCP path (#1633)
  • Superseded memories sync to mesh peers (#1665), pi lesson recall (#1605), npm run build on Windows cmd.exe (#1362), .env.example wording (#1675)

Packages

  • @agentmemory/pi 0.1.1: full lesson read by id, scoped to its project (#1605)
  • @agentmemory/fs-watcher 0.2.0: first watcher release since April, with preview redaction of private keys and JWTs, auth by default, confined paths and linked-worktree project scope

Known issues

  • Windows: stop takes about 60 s and empty console windows open per start (iii-hq/iii#2430)

Contributors

Thanks @cbsincool, @Tyxiel, @pei711, @mothieras, @akushonkamen, @dmazhukov, @reevesc88, @Rex57, @inix-x, @xh123637, @egantz, @YeChenglin8888 and @mattamundson for the fixes in this release, and to everyone who reported and diagnosed the Windows and boot issues.

Full changelog: https://github.com/rohitg00/agentmemory/compare/v0.9.31...v0.9.32

zed-industries/zed ↗ 2 releases · 1 stable · 1 prerelease
v1.27.0 Stable2026-11-04T17:04:23Z

This week's release includes syntax highlighting for Go 1.27 generic methods, an option to open README files by default, and AI commit messages that follow your repository's recent commit style.

Features

AI

  • Improved Agent Panel memory usage by loading tool-call diffs and parsing thread message Markdown only when first viewed, instead of when loading a thread. (#65444, #65470; thanks Vanuan)
  • Improved AI commit message generation by using the three most recent repository commit messages as style examples. (#61528; thanks matheuslrd)
  • Improved CPU usage and disk reads while the agent was working, especially with a long thread history. (#66015)

Git

  • Added missing tooltips to the close, commit message, and "View on" buttons in the Git Graph's commit details panel. (#66016; thanks skmanoj322)
  • Improved Git Graph rendering performance. (#66180; thanks RemcoSmitsDev)
  • Improved the Git Panel's error message when a push was rejected because the local branch was behind the remote, explaining that remote changes must be fetched and integrated before pushing again. (#47296; thanks Aladiah96)

Languages

  • Added syntax highlighting for Go 1.27 generic methods. (#64562; thanks ndellosa95)

Other

  • Added an option to open README files by default by setting on_new_window to "readme". (#65389)
  • Improved the Project Panel error message when moving a file or folder onto a location where one with the same name already existed. (#64344; thanks zaugust4891)

Bug Fixes

  • Fixed Solargraph completions crashing Zed in certain cases. (#66157)
  • Fixed "Zed quit unexpectedly" appearing when quitting with cmd-q on macOS. (#66158)
  • Fixed a crash when Tree-sitter syntax highlighting failed. (#66474)
  • Fixed a crash when adding surrounding characters (m s) to adjacent selections in Helix mode. (#66107; thanks bugprone)
  • Fixed a hang in Vim visual-block mode at the end of a wrapped file. (#66118)
  • Fixed child filesystem watches losing events when a covering parent watch was removed. (#65998)
  • Fixed collapsed inline diff hunks producing invalid word-diff highlights for multibyte UTF-8 text. (#65829; thanks Blazearth)
  • Fixed external agents' tool call locations opening one line below the line the agent reported. (#66110)
  • Fixed Git Graph rendering lagging behind when opening or closing the commit details panel. (#66132; thanks RemcoSmitsDev)
  • Fixed inline review switching to a subagent's edits when the subagent started. (#66093)
  • Fixed language server formatting requests sometimes applying incorrect changes. (#65856)
  • Fixed language server setup failing with EBADDEVENGINES in Yarn and pnpm projects. (#65280; thanks latin-1)
  • Fixed language servers receiving change and save notifications for files outside their dynamically registered document selectors, including unrelated files in the project. (#65253; thanks aviatesk)
  • Fixed LLM credentials being read when disable_ai was set to true. (#66029)
  • Fixed missed filesystem events for case-insensitive watches registered with trailing separators or equivalent path spellings. (#65995)
  • Fixed missing Git repository, worktree, and branch names in the title bar for Windows remote projects opened in a repository subfolder. (#66142; thanks lingyaochu)
  • Fixed missing thinking output from OpenAI-compatible Responses endpoints that emitted reasoning-text events. (#65938)
  • Fixed on-type formatting after pressing enter being applied before the new line's indentation, which left the cursor after text inserted by language servers such as Ruby LSP. (#65769; thanks JacobNWolf)
  • Fixed remote projects (SSH, WSL) becoming unresponsive when loading an agent thread that referred to many files outside the project. (#65189; thanks coinman-dev)
  • Fixed renaming files, creating files, and renaming terminal tabs being cancelled when focus_follows_mouse was enabled and the mouse moved. (#66167)
  • Fixed retryable provider failures in OpenAI-compatible chat streams not being recognized as retryable. (#66038)
  • Fixed right-click menus and popup pickers closing when the mouse moved to another pane with focus_follows_mouse enabled. (#66173)
  • Fixed sandboxed agent terminal commands incorrectly reporting failure under dash on Linux. (#64439; thanks NHendrickson9616)
  • Fixed SSH connections failing when usernames containing / or \ were percent-encoded. (#64744; thanks ryanabx)
  • Fixed status bar panel buttons doing nothing when another panel in the same dock was zoomed. (#66119; thanks ybelmach)
  • Fixed streaming connection failures being classified as non-retryable errors for Google AI and OpenAI Responses model requests. (#65940)
  • Fixed the automatically installed Delve debugger for Go not updating after its initial installation. (#66025)
  • Fixed changes to focus_follows_mouse not applying to docks until Zed was restarted. (#66161)
  • Fixed deleted files remaining in the File Finder's history. (#65660)
  • Fixed duplicating files or folders in the Project Panel overwriting clipboard contents. (#60894; thanks shindgew)
  • Fixed external stash renames not being reflected in the stash picker. (#55294)
  • Fixed failed model-list fetches, such as those caused by invalid or expired API keys, being silently dropped instead of logged for Anthropic, Ollama, LM Studio, llama.cpp, OpenRouter, and Vercel AI Gateway. (#64171; thanks somtri)
  • Fixed language servers' dynamically registered capabilities applying to files outside their document selector's pattern. (#65252; thanks aviatesk)
  • Fixed long recent project names and the Open Project shortcut overflowing on the Welcome screen and in narrow panels. (#65531; thanks junkmailkeep)
  • Fixed pasting in Helix select mode with shift-r, space p, or cmd-v not returning to normal mode. (#65492; thanks raum-dellamorte)
  • Fixed project entries reappearing in Recent Projects after removal. (#66127; thanks EcutAtom336)
  • Fixed project names in the title bar for repo.git bare clones on remote Windows hosts. (#66199; thanks lingyaochu)
  • Fixed reordering a Markdown editor tab switching back to Markdown preview when "Open Markdown Files in Preview" was enabled. (#65795; thanks mateioprea)
  • Fixed running Go tests on Windows failing with an invalid-directory error. (#65722; thanks giocld)
  • Fixed scrollbar settings not applying to already open terminals, panels, and menus until they were reopened. (#66017)
  • Fixed unnecessary-code dimming sometimes disappearing when code_lens and semantic_tokens: "full" were both enabled. (#56003)

Breaking Changes and Notices

  • Removed support for macOS Catalina and Big Sur. Zed now requires macOS 12.0 (Monterey) or later. (#65994)
  • Removed agent checkpoints and the "Restore Checkpoint" button. Taking a checkpoint before every message made the agent slow to respond, or hang, in large Git repositories. To undo the agent's changes, reject its edits or use Git. (#65989)

v1.28.0-pre Prerelease2026-11-04T17:02:18Z

This week's release includes optional automatic Git fetching, a resizable Refs column in the Git Graph, dragging Git Panel files into terminals and editors, and mouse-wheel font zoom in diff views and terminals.

Features

AI

  • Added an edit_predictions.button setting to toggle the edit prediction button in the status bar. (#66450; thanks z0al)
  • Improved compatibility with the latest Agent Client Protocol, including handling of missing sessions, plan updates, diff formats, and cancellation of permission requests. (#66425)
  • Improved the Agent's read_file tool output by truncating lines longer than 2,000 characters to prevent context window overflow. (#65395; thanks Antphar)
  • Improved subagent cards so that full screen opens a finished subagent run at its start. (#66252)

Git

  • Added optional automatic fetching of the current branch's upstream remote and the default branch's remote. Enable it with git.auto_fetch.enabled; configure the interval with git.auto_fetch.interval_secs (60 seconds by default). Automatic fetching is disabled by default. (#53089; thanks andriygm)
  • Added a resizable "Refs" column to the Git Graph that can be toggled on or off from the column header's context menu. (#66260; thanks RemcoSmitsDev)
  • Added support for dragging files from the Git Panel to the terminal and editor panes. (#66423; thanks mmatczuk)
  • Added support for searching by branch name in the worktree picker. (#66384; thanks michelinux)

Debugger

  • Added support for configuring the debugger panel’s default size via debugger.default_width and debugger.default_height. (#66435)

Languages

  • Improved call hierarchy readability by adding spacing between item names and details. (#66144; thanks yskim308)

Linux

  • Improved window startup time on Linux with NVIDIA GPUs. (#66002; thanks runsonmypc)

Other

  • Added support for mouse-wheel font zoom in diff views and terminals with mouse_wheel_zoom enabled: cmd-scroll on macOS and ctrl-scroll on Linux/Windows. (#66296; thanks ndri)
  • Improved responsiveness when navigating code, loading completion details, and comparing files. (#66487)
  • Improved the reliability of language server and agent downloads with consistent checksum verification, rejection of failed HTTP responses, and better cleanup of partial downloads. (#66048)

Bug Fixes

  • Fixed a bug where launching Zed on Windows while it was already running prevented the previous session from being restored. (#66364; thanks somtri)
  • Fixed a crash when Tree-sitter syntax highlighting failed. (#66474)
  • Fixed a hang in Vim visual-block mode at the end of a wrapped file. (#66118)
  • Fixed Agent context compaction unnecessarily using uncached input tokens with supported providers, including OpenAI and xAI. (#64938; thanks tidely)
  • Fixed auto-compaction only running once during a long agent turn, which could let the thread exceed the model's context window. (#66215)
  • Fixed compaction not reusing the prompt cache for ChatGPT subscription models. (#66527)
  • Fixed creating pull requests from Zed for Forgejo remotes, including Codeberg. (#66182; thanks Lachstec)
  • Fixed external agents sometimes starting without MCP servers configured in user settings when the thread was created before the project folder finished loading. (#64612; thanks philwinder)
  • Fixed Helix mode operations overwriting the system clipboard when vim.use_system_clipboard was set to "always". (#55455; thanks SrGaabriel)
  • Fixed local files opening as empty buffers in remote workspaces. (#65771; thanks Huliiiiii)
  • Fixed mentioning, sharing, and deleting project skills in remote workspaces. (#66207)
  • Fixed Project Search buttons affecting the wrong pane when clicked in an unfocused split. (#50882; thanks transitoryangel)
  • Fixed registry agents installed via npm (such as Claude Agent) failing to launch with ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND after an interrupted or concurrent install. (#66235)
  • Fixed remote terminals and tasks failing on Windows SSH servers whose default shell is Nushell. (#65580; thanks lingyaochu)
  • Fixed remote workspaces sharing a directory being merged into one workspace after restarting Zed. (#66289; thanks buvidk1234)
  • Fixed stale language server data being shown in multibuffers. (#66264)
  • Fixed SVG images failing to render in Markdown previews for remote projects. (#66404; thanks max1ab)
  • Fixed synthetic mouse movements on Windows repositioning the editor cursor when the window repainted, such as when opening the command palette. (#55739; thanks khaledtf19)
  • Fixed the Git Panel showing “No Git Repositories” after removing the active repository’s project folder from a remote workspace while other repositories remained open. (#66299; thanks buvidk1234)
  • Fixed the global busy cursor lingering after Zed opened on GNOME Wayland. (#64058; thanks eukreign)
  • Fixed the skill creator saving project skills to the local machine instead of the remote in remote workspaces. (#66224)
  • Fixed wide submenus and popovers covering the menu or button they open from. (#66496)
  • Fixed worktree task hooks running when a workspace was in restricted mode. (#66284)
  • Fixed a missing scrollbar in the Open Remote picker, making additional remote servers easier to discover. (#63909)
  • Fixed AI features and edit predictions continuing to use an organization after you were removed from it. (#66494)
  • Fixed an issue where using pane: reveal in project panel after closing the last terminal tab would unexpectedly reopen the terminal panel. (#66472)
  • Fixed author names and avatars being vertically misaligned in the Git Graph. (#66187; thanks albertbogusz)
  • Fixed call hierarchy hover tooltips not showing full details, including package paths, when rows were truncated. (#66144; thanks yskim308)
  • Fixed false missing-property warnings for CodeLLDB debug configurations using build-based target inference. (#64133; thanks ZhehooMorgen)
  • Fixed images in the Markdown preview being rendered with rounded corners. (#65474; thanks OmChillure)
  • Fixed missing syntax highlighting for C++ lambda specifiers and the defined preprocessor operator. (#66406; thanks riddhigupta1110)
  • Fixed Remote Projects rejecting SSH commands whose ssh executable was shell-quoted. (#66360; thanks zozo123)
  • Fixed square corners showing on terminal tool cards in the Agent Panel with themes whose element background differed from the panel background. (#66367; thanks fuzzcan)
  • Fixed the History tab in the Git Panel losing the highlight on the open commit when clicking into the commit view. (#66226)
  • Fixed Windows shell detection for executable names containing uppercase letters. (#66387; thanks M0-kii)
